\begin{DoxyItemize}

\item extern from/to\+\_\+json result in linker error \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/issues/3657}{\texttt{ \#3657}}
\item json\+\_\+fwd.\+hpp no longer standalone \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/issues/3656}{\texttt{ \#3656}}

\item Regression\+: no match for \textquotesingle{}operator!=\textquotesingle{} comparing \doxylink{classjson__pointer}{json\+\_\+pointer} and const char \texorpdfstring{$\ast$}{*}/string\+\_\+t \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/issues/3654}{\texttt{ \#3654}}

\item mac\+OS 10.\+15 Actions runner image deprecation \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/issues/3612}{\texttt{ \#3612}}

\item Add Python script for generating Natvis file and update file for 3.\+11.\+2 \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/pull/3697}{\texttt{ \#3697}} (\href{https://github.com/falbrechtskirchinger}{\texttt{ falbrechtskirchinger}})
\item fix typo in json\+\_\+pointer.\+md \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/pull/3692}{\texttt{ \#3692}} (\href{https://github.com/eltociear}{\texttt{ eltociear}})

\item Make \doxylink{classjson__pointer}{json\+\_\+pointer} usable as map key (again) \href{https://github.com/nlohmann/json/pull/3685}{\texttt{ \#3685}} (\href{https://github.com/falbrechtskirchinger}{\texttt{ falbrechtskirchinger}})
